/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba scripts/dontpanic_orchestrate/plan_review/scope_delta.py | sed -n '1,360p'" in /Users/bayesian/Documents/GitHub/DontPanic
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Mid-development scope-delta lint (F006).
     2	
     3	Keeps scope honest as a plan *evolves* — the moment most drift is introduced.
     4	On any change to ``features.json`` / ``objective_contract``, this re-runs the
     5	F001 lint on the **changed features only** (the diff, not the whole plan),
     6	classifies each change, and enforces the scope-change protocol:
     7	
     8	  * **sharpen** — an AC was narrowed / concretised with no new surface and no
     9	    new exemplar. Passes without friction (acceptance #5).
    10	  * **expand** — an AC or surface was *added* to a feature. When the feature is
    11	    locked AND the expand pushes it past the size budget, it is **refused**
    12	    until either a recorded rationale is supplied or the feature is split
    13	    (acceptance #3).
    14	  * **split** — one feature became several (children carry the parent's ACs).
    15	    Accepted only when AC conservation holds; a **lossy** split is refused,
    16	    naming the dropped / duplicated criteria (acceptance #4).
    17	
    18	Relationship to the other plan-review gates
    19	-------------------------------------------
    20	F004 (pre-lock) and F007 (pre-dispatch) gate a *static* snapshot. F006 gates a
    21	*transition* between two snapshots: it is the only gate that reasons about what
    22	changed, so it is the one that can tell a benign sharpen from a budget-busting
    23	expand and a clean split from a lossy one.
    24	
    25	Purity: everything here is a pure function over the prior + current feature
    26	lists. No network, no filesystem, no mutation. The CLI / edit hook that calls
    27	it owns reading the two snapshots and recording any rationale.
    28	"""
    29	
    30	from __future__ import annotations
    31	
    32	from collections import Counter
    33	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    34	from typing import Literal
    35	
    36	from dontpanic_orchestrate.plan_review.lint import (
    37	    Resolvers,
    38	    ScopeReport,
    39	    lint_feature,
    40	    split_acceptance,
    41	)
    42	from dontpanic_orchestrate.plan_review.sizing_gate import SIZE_FLAG_KINDS
    43	
    44	# Minimum non-whitespace length of a scope-change rationale that unblocks a
    45	# budget-busting expand. Mirrors the F004 / F007 / patch-completeness bar.
    46	MIN_REASON_LEN = 8
    47	
    48	ChangeKind = Literal["sharpen", "expand", "split"]
    49	
    50	
    51	# ─────────────────────────────── public types ──────────────────────────────
    52	
    53	
    54	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    55	class ScopeDelta:
    56	    """The classification + verdict for one changed feature.
    57	
    58	    ``refused`` is True only for a budget-busting expand without a rationale or
    59	    a lossy split — exactly the two scope-change-protocol refusal paths
    60	    (acceptance #3 / #4). A sharpen is never refused (#5).
    61	    """
    62	
    63	    feature_id: str
    64	    kind: ChangeKind
    65	    refused: bool
    66	    reason: str
    67	    evidence: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    68	
    69	    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    70	        return {
    71	            "feature_id": self.feature_id,
    72	            "kind": self.kind,
    73	            "refused": self.refused,
    74	            "reason": self.reason,
    75	            "evidence": dict(self.evidence),
    76	        }
    77	
    78	
    79	@dataclass
    80	class ScopeDeltaReport:
    81	    deltas: tuple[ScopeDelta, ...]
    82	
    83	    @property
    84	    def refusals(self) -> tuple[ScopeDelta, ...]:
    85	        return tuple(d for d in self.deltas if d.refused)
    86	
    87	    @property
    88	    def is_blocked(self) -> bool:
    89	        return bool(self.refusals)
    90	
    91	    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    92	        return {"deltas": [d.to_dict() for d in self.deltas]}
    93	
    94	
    95	# ─────────────────────────────── helpers ───────────────────────────────────
    96	
    97	
    98	def _by_id(features: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:
    99	    return {str(f["id"]): f for f in features if f.get("id")}
   100	
   101	
   102	def _acceptance_criteria(feature: dict) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   103	    """The feature's acceptance criteria as a normalized tuple (reuses F001's
   104	    ``split_acceptance`` so conservation is compared the same way F002 does)."""
   105	    acc = feature.get("acceptance")
   106	    if isinstance(acc, (list, tuple)):
   107	        acc = "\n".join(str(a) for a in acc)
   108	    return split_acceptance(str(acc or ""))
   109	
   110	
   111	def _has_block_size_flag(report: ScopeReport) -> bool:
   112	    return any(
   113	        f.severity == "block" and f.kind in SIZE_FLAG_KINDS for f in report.flags
   114	    )
   115	
   116	
   117	def _has_exemplar(report: ScopeReport) -> bool:
   118	    return any(f.kind == "exemplar_ac" for f in report.flags)
   119	
   120	
   121	def changed_feature_ids(prior: list[dict], current: list[dict]) -> set[str]:
   122	    """Ids whose record differs between the two snapshots, plus ids present in
   123	    only one snapshot. This is the set the lint re-runs on (acceptance #1 — the
   124	    changed features only, never the whole plan)."""
   125	    prior_map, cur_map = _by_id(prior), _by_id(current)
   126	    changed: set[str] = set()
   127	    for fid in prior_map.keys() | cur_map.keys():
   128	        if prior_map.get(fid) != cur_map.get(fid):
   129	            changed.add(fid)
   130	    return changed
   131	
   132	
   133	# ─────────────────────────────── classifiers ───────────────────────────────
   134	
   135	
   136	def _classify_modify(
   137	    prior_feat: dict,
   138	    cur_feat: dict,
   139	    *,
   140	    locked: bool,
   141	    rationale: str | None,
   142	    resolvers: Resolvers | None,
   143	) -> ScopeDelta:
   144	    """Classify a feature present in BOTH snapshots as sharpen or expand, and
   145	    decide the expand refusal."""
   146	    fid = str(cur_feat.get("id") or prior_feat.get("id") or "(unnamed)")
   147	    prior_report = lint_feature(prior_feat, resolvers)
   148	    cur_report = lint_feature(cur_feat, resolvers)
   149	
   150	    added_surfaces = sorted(set(cur_report.surfaces) - set(prior_report.surfaces))
   151	    added_acs = cur_report.ac_count - prior_report.ac_count
   152	    new_exemplar = _has_exemplar(cur_report) and not _has_exemplar(prior_report)
   153	
   154	    is_expand = bool(added_surfaces) or added_acs > 0
   155	    evidence = {
   156	        "added_surfaces": added_surfaces,
   157	        "added_acs": added_acs,
   158	        "prior_ac_count": prior_report.ac_count,
   159	        "current_ac_count": cur_report.ac_count,
   160	        "new_exemplar": new_exemplar,
   161	    }
   162	
   163	    if not is_expand:
   164	        # sharpen — narrowed/concretised, no new surface, no added ACs.
   165	        return ScopeDelta(
   166	            feature_id=fid,
   167	            kind="sharpen",
   168	            refused=False,
   169	            reason=(
   170	                f"{fid}: sharpen — no surface added, AC count "
   171	                f"{prior_report.ac_count}→{cur_report.ac_count}; passes without friction."
   172	            ),
   173	            evidence=evidence,
   174	        )
   175	
   176	    # expand. Refuse only when it pushes a LOCKED feature past the size budget
   177	    # and no rationale was recorded (acceptance #3).
   178	    crosses_budget = _has_block_size_flag(cur_report)
   179	    evidence["crosses_size_budget"] = crosses_budget
   180	    has_rationale = rationale is not None and len(rationale.strip()) >= MIN_REASON_LEN
   181	    if has_rationale:
   182	        evidence["scope_change_rationale"] = rationale
   183	
   184	    refused = locked and crosses_budget and not has_rationale
   185	    if refused:
   186	        reason = (
   187	            f"{fid}: expand REFUSED — a locked feature was pushed past the size "
   188	            f"budget (added surfaces={added_surfaces or '∅'}, added ACs={added_acs}). "
   189	            "Scope-change protocol: add a recorded rationale (>=8 chars) OR split "
   190	            "the feature before this expand can land."
   191	        )
   192	    elif locked and crosses_budget and has_rationale:
   193	        reason = (
   194	            f"{fid}: expand allowed with recorded rationale despite crossing the "
   195	            f"size budget (added surfaces={added_surfaces or '∅'}, added ACs={added_acs})."
   196	        )
   197	    else:
   198	        reason = (
   199	            f"{fid}: expand within budget (added surfaces={added_surfaces or '∅'}, "
   200	            f"added ACs={added_acs})."
   201	        )
   202	    return ScopeDelta(
   203	        feature_id=fid, kind="expand", refused=refused, reason=reason, evidence=evidence
   204	    )
   205	
   206	
   207	def _classify_split(
   208	    parent_feat: dict,
   209	    children: list[dict],
   210	) -> ScopeDelta:
   211	    """Classify a parent feature that became several children, verifying AC
   212	    conservation (acceptance #4). A lossy split is refused, naming the dropped
   213	    and duplicated criteria."""
   214	    parent_id = str(parent_feat.get("id") or "(unnamed)")
   215	    parent_ms = Counter(_acceptance_criteria(parent_feat))
   216	    child_ms: Counter[str] = Counter()
   217	    for child in children:
   218	        child_ms.update(_acceptance_criteria(child))
   219	
   220	    dropped = sorted((parent_ms - child_ms).elements())
   221	    duplicated = sorted((child_ms - parent_ms).elements())
   222	    conserved = parent_ms == child_ms
   223	
   224	    child_ids = [str(c.get("id") or "(unnamed)") for c in children]
   225	    evidence = {
   226	        "child_ids": child_ids,
   227	        "dropped": dropped,
   228	        "duplicated": duplicated,
   229	        "conservation_ok": conserved,
   230	    }
   231	    if conserved:
   232	        reason = (
   233	            f"{parent_id}: split into {len(children)} feature(s) "
   234	            f"[{', '.join(child_ids)}] — AC conservation holds (zero dropped, "
   235	            "zero duplicated)."
   236	        )
   237	        return ScopeDelta(
   238	            feature_id=parent_id,
   239	            kind="split",
   240	            refused=False,
   241	            reason=reason,
   242	            evidence=evidence,
   243	        )
   244	    reason = (
   245	        f"{parent_id}: split REFUSED — lossy partition. "
   246	        f"dropped={dropped or '∅'}; duplicated={duplicated or '∅'}. "
   247	        "A split must reproduce the parent's acceptance criteria exactly."
   248	    )
   249	    return ScopeDelta(
   250	        feature_id=parent_id,
   251	        kind="split",
   252	        refused=True,
   253	        reason=reason,
   254	        evidence=evidence,
   255	    )
   256	
   257	
   258	# ─────────────────────────────── public API ────────────────────────────────
   259	
   260	
   261	def review_scope_delta(
   262	    prior_features: list[dict],
   263	    current_features: list[dict],
   264	    *,
   265	    locked_ids: frozenset[str] | set[str] = frozenset(),
   266	    rationales: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   267	    resolvers: Resolvers | None = None,
   268	) -> ScopeDeltaReport:
   269	    """Classify every changed feature between two plan snapshots and apply the
   270	    scope-change protocol.
   271	
   272	    A **split** is signalled by child features carrying a ``split_of: <parent>``
   273	    annotation whose parent is absent from ``current_features`` — the conserving
   274	    set is then verified. A feature present in both snapshots is classified as
   275	    sharpen or expand. ``rationales`` maps a feature id to an operator-supplied
   276	    scope-change rationale that unblocks a budget-busting expand.
   277	    """
   278	    rationales = rationales or {}
   279	    prior_map = _by_id(prior_features)
   280	    cur_map = _by_id(current_features)
   281	
   282	    # Group split children by their declared parent.
   283	    split_children: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   284	    for feat in current_features:
   285	        parent = feat.get("split_of")
   286	        if parent:
   287	            split_children.setdefault(str(parent), []).append(feat)
   288	
   289	    deltas: list[ScopeDelta] = []
   290	    handled: set[str] = set()
   291	
   292	    # 1. Splits: a prior feature gone from current, with declared children.
   293	    for parent_id, children in split_children.items():
   294	        if parent_id in prior_map and parent_id not in cur_map:
   295	            deltas.append(_classify_split(prior_map[parent_id], children))
   296	            handled.add(parent_id)
   297	
   298	    # 2. Modified features present in both snapshots.
   299	    for fid in changed_feature_ids(prior_features, current_features):
   300	        if fid in handled:
   301	            continue
   302	        prior_feat = prior_map.get(fid)
   303	        cur_feat = cur_map.get(fid)
   304	        if prior_feat is None or cur_feat is None:
   305	            # A pure add (child of a split is handled above) or a pure removal
   306	            # is not one of the three in-scope change classes; skip silently.
   307	            continue
   308	        deltas.append(
   309	            _classify_modify(
   310	                prior_feat,
   311	                cur_feat,
   312	                locked=fid in locked_ids,
   313	                rationale=rationales.get(fid),
   314	                resolvers=resolvers,
   315	            )
   316	        )
   317	
   318	    deltas.sort(key=lambda d: d.feature_id)
   319	    return ScopeDeltaReport(deltas=tuple(deltas))
   320	
   321	
   322	def render_block_message(report: ScopeDeltaReport) -> str:
   323	    """Refusal message naming every refused delta (the scope-change-protocol
   324	    violations) — the budget-busting expands and the lossy splits."""
   325	    if not report.is_blocked:
   326	        return "[scope-delta] no scope-change-protocol violations."
   327	    lines = [
   328	        "[scope-delta] BLOCKED by the scope-change protocol — the following "
   329	        "changes are refused:",
   330	    ]
   331	    for delta in report.refusals:
   332	        lines.append(f"  [{delta.kind}] {delta.reason}")
   333	    return "\n".join(lines)

   109	returns ``None`` unless at least one parsed line carries a ``type`` in
   110	this set. Arbitrary JSONL whose objects happen to be parseable per-line
   111	but carry no recognized event ``type`` is NOT treated as a codex stream
   112	(it falls through to the existing raw-JSON path)."""
   113	
   114	_DISPATCH_FEATURE_ID: str = "F002"
   115	"""F2/F002 is the plan-level dispatcher; the dispatched executor task
   116	carries this feature_id so downstream tooling that keys on feature_id
   117	(env registry, target-context cross-check) sees a stable identifier."""
   118	
   119	
   120	_AuditStatus = Literal[
   121	    "agree",
   122	    "disagree",
   123	    "dispatch_skipped_offline",
   124	    "dispatch_response_malformed",
   125	]
   126	
   127	
   128	_SEVERITY_DISPOSITIONS: tuple[str, ...] = ("agree", "lower", "higher", "no_finding")
   129

   134	class SameVendorRefused(SufficiencyAuditError):
   135	    """Raised by :func:`dispatch_completion_audit` when the resolved
   136	    auditor matches the implementer and
   137	    :data:`_SAME_VENDOR_OVERRIDE_ENV` is unset.
   138	
   139	    Subclasses :class:`sufficiency_auditor.SufficiencyAuditError` so
   140	    callers (F003 plan-close gate) can catch the F1 base for one
   141	    ``except`` clause across both pre-impl and post-impl boundaries."""
   142	
   143	
   144	class CompletionDispatchError(RuntimeError):
   145	    """Raised when the dispatcher cannot complete a real dispatch run
   146	    for reasons unrelated to vendor resolution: resolved executor not
   147	    on PATH, prompt template missing, etc. Distinct from
   148	    :class:`SameVendorRefused` (which is a vendor-policy refusal,
   149	    not an availability failure)."""
   150	
   151	
   152	class ConfigNotReady(CompletionDispatchError):
   153	    """Plan 2026-06-01-001 F009 — raised when the pre-flight config-readiness
   154	    check (quota caps + role config) fails BEFORE the plan-close goal-completion
   155	    audit spends a paid call. Carries the actionable readiness message (the
   156	    file, the precise reason, and a runnable remediation command) so the
   157	    operator is never stranded by a raw ``{}``-style schema crash at plan-close,
   158	    mirroring the dispatch-from-plan readiness gate."""
   159

   163	
   164	class FindingDisposition(BaseModel):
   165	    """One auditor disposition keyed to a v1 :class:`CompletionFinding`.
   166	
   167	    The auditor responds with a list of these — one per v1 finding,
   168	    plus optional ``auditor-overlay-NNN`` entries when it spots a
   169	    coverage gap the v1 heuristic missed entirely (allowed because
   170	    v1 is intentionally simple per D002)."""
   171	
   172	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
   173	
   174	    finding_id: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   175	    agree: bool
   176	    severity_disposition: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   177	    comment: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   178	
   179	    @field_validator("severity_disposition")
   180	    @classmethod
   181	    def _validate_severity_disposition(cls, value: str) -> str:
   182	        if value not in _SEVERITY_DISPOSITIONS:
   183	            raise ValueError(
   184	                f"unknown severity_disposition: {value!r}; must be one of {_SEVERITY_DISPOSITIONS}"
   185	            )
   186	        return value
   187

   189	class CompletionAuditTranscript(BaseModel):
   190	    """Envelope returned by :func:`dispatch_completion_audit` and serialized

 succeeded in 0ms:
   190	    """Envelope returned by :func:`dispatch_completion_audit` and serialized
   191	    to ``evidence/goal-governance/post_impl/audit/audit-<auditor>-<iter>.json``.
   192	
   193	    ``status`` is the load-bearing decision field consumed by F003's
   194	    plan-close gate:
   195	
   196	    - ``agree``: every disposition's ``agree=True`` — F2 findings are
   197	      authoritative.
   198	    - ``disagree``: at least one disposition disagrees — F003 refuses
   199	      status flip until the operator either resolves the disagreement
   200	      or invokes the input-bound override.
   201	    - ``dispatch_skipped_offline``: synthetic envelope produced when
   202	      :data:`_OFFLINE_ENV` is set; no executor was invoked.
   203	    - ``dispatch_response_malformed``: auditor responded with text the
   204	      parser could not interpret as a JSON disposition list. F003
   205	      treats this as block; operator overrides or fixes."""
   206	
   207	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
   208	
   209	    auditor_agent: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   210	    implementer_agent: str | None = None
   211	    status: _AuditStatus
   212	    iteration: int = Field(..., ge=0)
   213	    findings_dispositions: list[FindingDisposition] = Field(default_factory=list)
   214	    transcript_path: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   215	    envelope_path: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   216	    generated_at: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
   217	    raw_response: str | None = None

   220	# ──────────────────────────────  test-injection seam  ──────────────────────────────

   222	
   223	DispatchFn = Callable[[str, str], str]
   224	"""Test-injection seam for :func:`dispatch_completion_audit`.
   225	
   226	Signature: ``dispatch(auditor_agent, prompt) -> raw_response``.
   227	
   228	Mirrors F1's :data:`sufficiency_auditor.DispatchFn` shape so test
   229	stubs can be reused across both audit boundaries. When ``dispatch`` is
   230	``None`` (production), the dispatcher uses
   231	:func:`executors.get_executor` against
   232	:data:`executors.AGENT_REGISTRY` to invoke the resolved auditor's CLI.
   233	"""

   239	def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
   240	    """Deterministic ISO8601 UTC timestamp (suffix ``+00:00``)."""
   241	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   242	
   243	
   244	def _resolve_auditor_or_translate_same_vendor(
   245	    plan_dir: Path,
   246	    implementer_agent: str | None,
   247	) -> str:
   248	    """Wrap :func:`_resolve_goal_auditor_agent` and translate F1's
   249	    same-vendor :class:`SufficiencyAuditError` into the cleaner
   250	    :class:`SameVendorRefused` subclass for caller ergonomics.
   251	
   252	    All other configuration errors propagate as
   253	    :class:`SufficiencyAuditError` (no goal_auditor configured, etc.),
   254	    so F003 can map a single base exception to its exit-5 vendor-error
   255	    bucket without ambiguity."""
   256	    try:
   257	        return _resolve_goal_auditor_agent(plan_dir, implementer_agent=implementer_agent)
   258	    except SufficiencyAuditError as exc:
   259	        if "cross-vendor invariant" in str(exc):
   260	            raise SameVendorRefused(str(exc)) from exc
   261	        raise
   262	
   263	
   264	def _load_features(plan_dir: Path) -> list[dict]:
   265	    """Read ``features.json`` and return the ``features`` list. Mirrors
   266	    F1's helper of the same name; kept inline rather than importing to
   267	    keep the boundary explicit (F2 does not depend on F1's private
   268	    helper surface)."""
   269	    features_json = plan_dir / "features.json"
   270	    if not features_json.is_file():
   271	        raise CompletionAuditError(f"features.json not found at {features_json}")
   272	    try:
   273	        raw = json.loads(features_json.read_text())
   274	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   275	        raise CompletionAuditError(f"{features_json}: failed to read: {exc}") from exc
   276	    items = raw.get("features") if isinstance(raw, dict) else None
   277	    if not isinstance(items, list):
   278	        raise CompletionAuditError(
   279	            f"{features_json}: expected top-level mapping with 'features' list"
   280	        )
   281	    return items
   282	
   283	
   284	def _read_prompt_template() -> str:
   285	    """Read the markdown template once per call.
   286	
   287	    Re-reading is cheap (small file, infrequent call) and avoids the
   288	    test-time hazard of caching a file the test fixture replaced."""
   289	    if not _PROMPT_TEMPLATE_PATH.is_file():
   290	        raise CompletionDispatchError(
   291	            f"completion-audit prompt template missing at {_PROMPT_TEMPLATE_PATH}"
   292	        )
   293	    return _PROMPT_TEMPLATE_PATH.read_text()
   294	
   295	
   296	def _serialize_contract(contract) -> str:
   297	    """Deterministic JSON-ish serialization of the ObjectiveContract for
   298	    the prompt body. ``model_dump_json`` returns sorted keys when we
   299	    pass through ``json.dumps(sort_keys=True)``."""
   300	    raw = contract.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
   301	    return json.dumps(raw, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
   302	
   303	
   304	def _serialize_features(features: list[dict]) -> str:
   305	    """Sorted-key JSON dump of the features list."""
   306	    return json.dumps(features, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
   307	
   308	
   309	def _serialize_findings(findings: Iterable[CompletionFinding]) -> str:
   310	    """Sorted-key JSON dump of the v1 findings."""
   311	    payload = [f.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True) for f in findings]
   312	    return json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
   313	
   314	
   315	def _serialize_manifest(manifest) -> str:
   316	    """Sorted-key JSON dump of the rebuilt evidence manifest. Each
   317	    EvidenceRef serializes via its Pydantic model_dump."""
   318	    payload = [ref.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True) for ref in manifest]
   319	    payload.sort(key=lambda r: (r.get("uri") or "", r.get("hash") or ""))
   320	    return json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
   321	
   322	
   323	def _build_audit_prompt(
   324	    contract,
   325	    features: list[dict],
   326	    findings: Iterable[CompletionFinding],
   327	    manifest,
   328	) -> str:
   329	    """Render the prompt template by substituting the four context
   330	    blocks. Uses ``str.replace`` rather than ``str.format`` so embedded
   331	    ``{`` / ``}`` characters in the JSON payloads do not collide with
   332	    the template's placeholders."""
   333	    template = _read_prompt_template()
   334	    return (
   335	        template.replace("{contract}", _serialize_contract(contract))
   336	        .replace("{features}", _serialize_features(features))
   337	        .replace("{findings}", _serialize_findings(findings))
   338	        .replace("{evidence_manifest}", _serialize_manifest(manifest))
   339	    )
   340	
   341	
   342	def _strip_code_fence(text: str) -> str:
   343	    """Tolerate ``` fenced output the same way F1's parser does. The
   344	    prompt asks for raw JSON; agents commonly fence anyway."""
   345	    stripped = text.strip()
   346	    if stripped.startswith("```"):
   347	        first_newline = stripped.find("\n")
   348	        if first_newline != -1:
   349	            inner = stripped[first_newline + 1 :]
   350	            if inner.endswith("```"):
   351	                inner = inner[: -len("```")]
   352	            return inner.strip()
   353	    return stripped
   354

   670	def _assert_config_ready_for_completion(
   671	    *,
   672	    implementer_agent: str | None,
   673	    auditor: str,
   674	    caps_path: Path | None = None,
   675	) -> None:
   676	    """Plan 2026-06-01-001 F009 — raise :class:`ConfigNotReady` if the quota-caps
   677	    config or the resolved role values are not usable, BEFORE the plan-close
   678	    goal-completion audit spends a paid call. Reuses the same readiness module
   679	    as the dispatch-from-plan gate so the actionable message + runnable
   680	    remediation are identical at both pre-flight sites."""
   681	    from dontpanic_orchestrate import config_readiness
   682	    from dontpanic_orchestrate.executors import AGENT_REGISTRY
   683	
   684	    roles = [r for r in (implementer_agent, auditor) if r]
   685	    result = config_readiness.check_config_readiness(
   686	        roles=roles,
   687	        registered_executors=set(AGENT_REGISTRY),
   688	        caps_path=caps_path,
   689	    )
   690	    if not result.ok:
   691	        raise ConfigNotReady(result.render())

   693	
   694	def dispatch_completion_audit(
   695	    plan_dir: Path,
   696	    *,
   697	    findings: list[CompletionFinding],
   698	    implementer_agent: str | None = None,
   699	    iteration: int = 1,
   700	    dispatch: DispatchFn | None = None,
   701	    caps_path: Path | None = None,
   702	) -> CompletionAuditTranscript:
   703	    """Run a cross-vendor goal audit on a v1 completion-findings list.
   704	
   705	    Resolution order (mirrors F1's resolver):
   706	
   707	    1. :func:`_resolve_goal_auditor_agent` walks
   708	       project-config → global-config → fallback per D004 of Plan G F006
   709	       and returns the resolved ``roles.goal_auditor``.
   710	    2. Same-vendor refusal (``effective_implementer == auditor`` AND
   711	       :data:`_SAME_VENDOR_OVERRIDE_ENV` unset) fail-fast as
   712	       :class:`SameVendorRefused` BEFORE building any prompt or writing
   713	       any disk surface.
   714	    3. :data:`_OFFLINE_ENV` truthy → synthetic
   715	       ``dispatch_skipped_offline`` envelope; no executor invoked.
   716	    4. Otherwise: build prompt + dispatch via injected ``dispatch=``
   717	       (test seam) OR :func:`get_executor` (production) → parse
   718	       response → write transcript + envelope.
   719	
   720	    The ``dispatch=`` seam is the test boundary; production path always
   721	    routes through :data:`executors.AGENT_REGISTRY` so the resolved
   722	    agent IS the agent invoked.
   723	    """
   724	    plan_dir = Path(plan_dir).resolve()
   725	    if not plan_dir.is_dir():
   726	        raise CompletionDispatchError(f"plan_dir does not exist: {plan_dir}")
   727	
   728	    auditor = _resolve_auditor_or_translate_same_vendor(plan_dir, implementer_agent)
   729	    _validate_auditor_name(auditor)
   730	
   731	    if _is_truthy_env(os.environ.get(_OFFLINE_ENV)):
   732	        return _emit_offline_envelope(plan_dir, auditor, iteration, implementer_agent)
   733	
   734	    contract = _load_objective_contract(plan_dir)
   735	    features = _load_features(plan_dir)
   736	    manifest = _build_evidence_manifest(plan_dir)
   737	    prompt = _build_audit_prompt(contract, features, findings, manifest)
   738	
   739	    if dispatch is not None:
   740	        raw_response = dispatch(auditor, prompt)
   741	    else:
   742	        # Plan 2026-06-01-001 F009 — config-readiness pre-flight before the
   743	        # real paid plan-close goal-completion audit (acceptance #1 names this
   744	        # site alongside dispatch). Validates quota caps AND the resolved role
   745	        # values; a malformed `{}` caps file (D039) or a bad role (D065) becomes
   746	        # a clean ConfigNotReady with a runnable remediation, never a raw schema
   747	        # crash mid-audit. The injected ``dispatch=`` test seam is NOT paid work
   748	        # and intentionally bypasses this gate.
   749	        _assert_config_ready_for_completion(
   750	            implementer_agent=implementer_agent, auditor=auditor, caps_path=caps_path
   751	        )
   752	        raw_response = _dispatch_via_executor(auditor, prompt, plan_dir=plan_dir)
   753	
   754	    status, dispositions = _parse_audit_response(raw_response, findings)
   755	
   756	    transcript_path = _transcript_path(plan_dir, auditor, iteration)
   757	    envelope_path = _envelope_path(plan_dir, auditor, iteration)
   758	    transcript = CompletionAuditTranscript(
   759	        auditor_agent=auditor,
   760	        implementer_agent=implementer_agent,

exec

   179	    return RoleResolution(role, value, "fallback", agent_surface.is_worker_capable(value))

   230	    if value is None:

   233	
   234	
   235	def _resolve_goal_auditor_agent(
   236	    plan_dir: Path,
   237	    implementer_agent: str | None = None,
   238	) -> str:
   239	    """Resolve the goal auditor agent for a plan, honoring D006's
   240	    cross-vendor invariant.
   241	
   242	    Resolution path:
   243	
   244	    1. Resolve project context for ``plan_dir`` via
   245	       :func:`project_config.find_project_for_plan_dir`. ``None`` when
   246	       the plan lives outside any registered project — falls through
   247	       to host-local defaults.
   248	    2. Call :func:`project_config.resolve_dispatch_defaults` to walk the
   249	       D004 precedence (project config → global config → hardcoded
   250	       fallback claude/codex). Returns the canonical ``implementer`` and
   251	       ``auditor`` strings.
   252	    3. Override the resolved implementer with ``implementer_agent`` when
   253	       the caller supplies one (lets a runtime override drive the
   254	       cross-vendor check without mutating config).
   255	    4. If ``effective_implementer == auditor`` (same-vendor), refuse
   256	       unless :data:`_SAME_VENDOR_OVERRIDE_ENV` is set truthy in the
   257	       environment. Same-vendor adversarial review is the antipattern
   258	       Goal Governance V1 §5 explicitly bans by default.
   259	
   260	    Returns the auditor agent name (e.g. ``'codex'``). Raises
   261	    :class:`ValueError` (specifically :class:`SufficiencyAuditError`)
   262	    when the cross-vendor invariant is violated without override.
   263	    """

   150	
   151	def derive_ownership_map(features: Iterable[Mapping]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
   152	    """Build a ``feature_id -> {owned path token}`` map from the plan.
   153	
   154	    An explicit ``owned_paths`` list on a feature record wins; otherwise the
   155	    owned set is the file-path tokens named in the feature's
   156	    description/steps/acceptance text. Pure — never mutates ``features``.
   157	    Features without an ``id`` are skipped.
   158	    """
   159	    result: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   160	    for feature in features:
   161	        fid = feature.get("id")
   162	        if not fid:
   163	            continue
   164	        explicit = feature.get("owned_paths")
   165	        if explicit:
   166	            owned = {_normalize(p) for p in explicit if _normalize(p)}
   167	        else:
   168	            owned = _extract_path_tokens(_feature_text(feature))
   169	        result[str(fid)] = owned
   170	    return result
   171	
   172	
   173	def _path_matches_token(repo_path: str, token: str) -> bool:
   174	    """True iff git ``repo_path`` is, or ends with, owned ``token``.
   175	
   176	    Ownership tokens extracted from prose are often partial (``cli.py``,
   177	    ``dashboard/app.js``) while git paths are full and source-root prefixed
   178	    (``scripts/dontpanic_orchestrate/cli.py``). A token matches on an exact
   179	    equality OR a path-component-aligned suffix (``.../<token>``) so a partial
   180	    token still resolves to the real file, while ``my_cli.py`` does NOT match
   181	    token ``cli.py`` (the boundary ``/`` is required)."""
   182	    rp = _normalize(repo_path)
   183	    tk = _normalize(token)
   184	    if not rp or not tk:
   185	        return False
   186	    return rp == tk or rp.endswith("/" + tk)
   187	
   188	
   189	def check_cross_feature_edit(
   190	    touched_paths: Iterable[str],
   191	    current_feature_id: str,
   192	    ownership_map: Mapping[str, Iterable[str]],
   193	    *,
   194	    acknowledged_paths: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   195	) -> list[CrossFeatureFinding]:
   196	    """Return one finding per foreign feature whose owned paths were touched.
   197	
   198	    A touched path is a bleed iff it matches a *foreign* feature's owned token
   199	    AND does NOT match the current feature's owned tokens (co-owned/shared paths
   200	    are never flagged) AND is not in ``acknowledged_paths``. Pure.
   201	    """
   202	    ack = {_normalize(a) for a in (acknowledged_paths or []) if _normalize(a)}
   203	    own_tokens = set(ownership_map.get(current_feature_id, set()))
   204	
   205	    foreign_hits: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   206	    for raw in touched_paths:
   207	        path = _normalize(raw)
   208	        if not path:
   209	            continue
   210	        # The current feature's own (or co-owned) paths are never a bleed.
   211	        if any(_path_matches_token(path, t) for t in own_tokens):
   212	            continue
   213	        # Operator-acknowledged shared edits are exempt.
   214	        if path in ack or any(_path_matches_token(path, a) for a in ack):
   215	            continue
   216	        for fid, tokens in ownership_map.items():
   217	            if fid == current_feature_id:
   218	                continue
   219	            if any(_path_matches_token(path, t) for t in tokens):
   220	                foreign_hits.setdefault(fid, set()).add(path)
   221	
   222	    return [
   223	        CrossFeatureFinding(
   224	            current_feature_id=current_feature_id,
   225	            foreign_feature_id=fid,
   226	            paths=tuple(sorted(foreign_hits[fid])),
   227	        )
   228	        for fid in sorted(foreign_hits)
   229	    ]
   230	
   231

codex
### F008: needs_changes

- Acceptance (2)/(4): co-owned/shared paths are silently exempted before foreign ownership is checked. In `cross_feature.py:210-212`, any path matching the current feature’s tokens is skipped, even if it is also owned by another feature. The test at `test_cross_feature_f008.py:123-130` locks in that bypass. Literal AC says a path owned by a different feature yields a block unless acknowledged; shared-file legitimacy should pass via recorded rationale, not silent allow.
- Acceptance (2)/(3): supervisor adds `loaded.plan.affected_paths` into `touched` at `supervisor.py:1415` and `supervisor.py:1445`. `affected_paths` is a plan declaration, not the dispatch diff. This can produce false `cross_feature_edit` findings for paths not actually touched by the patch, violating the “diff touching” wording and the clean own-path case.

### F006: needs_changes

- Acceptance (1): no plan-change wiring exists. `review_scope_delta` / `changed_feature_ids` are only exported and tested; `rg` shows no caller on `features.json` or `objective_contract` changes. The feature is a pure helper, not a gate that runs “on a plan change.”
- Acceptance (2): not every change is classified. `changed_feature_ids` includes added/removed feature ids, but `review_scope_delta` skips `prior_feat is None or cur_feat is None` at `scope_delta.py:302-307`, returning no delta for a pure add/removal.
- Acceptance (2)/(5): a changed feature that adds an exemplar but no surface/AC count is classified as frictionless `sharpen`. `new_exemplar` is computed at `scope_delta.py:152` but ignored by `is_expand` at `scope_delta.py:154`; a probe returned `('sharpen', False, {'new_exemplar': True})`. AC5 only grants frictionless sharpen when no surface and no exemplar are added.

### F005: needs_changes

- Acceptance (1): this does not run through `dispatch_volley`. `run_design_volley` calls `auditor.dispatch(...)` and `planner.dispatch(...)` directly at `design_review.py:262` and `design_review.py:314`. The docstring says it “mirrors” `dispatch_volley`, but AC requires it to run through it.
- Acceptance (3): no `pre_lock` opt-in wiring exists. `should_run_design_volley` is only used in tests/exported symbols; there is no pre-lock caller, no operator request surface, and no connection from F001 lint uncertainty to an actual volley trigger.

### F009: needs_changes

- Acceptance (1)/(2): plan-close still validates the resolved auditor name before the readiness gate. `dispatch_completion_audit` resolves and calls `_validate_auditor_name(auditor)` at `completion_dispatch.py:728-729`; `_assert_config_ready_for_completion` only runs later at `completion_dispatch.py:749-751`. A configured bad auditor like `Codex-Auditor` can therefore raise the old `CompletionDispatchError` at `completion_dispatch.py:551-558`, not the clean `ConfigNotReady` readiness failure with file/reason/remediation/dashboard.
- Acceptance (5): the plan-close invalid-role test does not reproduce the real role-config path. It calls `_assert_config_ready_for_completion` directly with bad arguments at `test_completion_config_readiness_f009.py:44-51`, but does not assert a plan-close invocation with invalid `~/.dontpanic/config.json` / legacy fallback, validated remediation tokens, or dashboard affordance.
